       (b) properties of, or relations among, THINGS IN THEMSELVES
           (LEIBNIZ's relational view) - REJECTED
       (c) empirical concepts abstracted from experience, since we need
           space and time ALREADY IN PLACE to have any experience at all
        |
        v
THE ASYMMETRY THAT DOES ALL THE LATER WORK
   OUTER representations are in SPACE and, being MINE, also in TIME
   INNER representations are in TIME ONLY
   ==> TIME IS THE FORM OF *ALL* REPRESENTATIONS; SPACE IS NOT
   ==> only TIME can supply the mediating SCHEMA (see Schematism panel)
        |
        v
PLAIN TEST OF FORM VERSUS CONTENT
   You can think a space empty of objects; you cannot think space away.
   You can think a stretch of time in which nothing happens; you cannot
   think an experience that is at NO time.
   Whatever can be subtracted is CONTENT. Whatever cannot be subtracted
   is FORM.
TRAP: treating space and time symmetrically. The Schematism depends on
   the asymmetry, and stating it in the Aesthetic then using it later
   demonstrates architecture rather than recall.

ASCII MASTER FLOW — PANEL 16/74: The metaphysical exposition - four arguments, two jobs
==============================================================================
ARG 1  NOT DERIVED FROM EXPERIENCE
   To refer sensations to something "OUTSIDE ME", and to represent
   things as in different places "beside one another", the
   representation of space must ALREADY be presupposed. Space is
   therefore PRESUPPOSED BY outer experience, not abstracted from it.
                                                  ==> A PRIORI
ARG 2  NECESSARY
   We can never represent to ourselves that there is no space, though we
   can very well think that NO OBJECTS are encountered in it. Space is
   the condition of the possibility of appearances, not a determination
   dependent on them.                             ==> A PRIORI
ARG 3  SINGULAR, NOT A DISCURSIVE CONCEPT
   We can represent only ONE space; the many "spaces" are LIMITATIONS
   within it. A general concept has particulars falling UNDER it,
   whereas the parts of space are IN it and are given only through
   limitation.                                    ==> INTUITION
ARG 4  GIVEN AS AN INFINITE MAGNITUDE
   Space is represented as an infinite GIVEN magnitude. A concept can
   have infinitely many representations UNDER it, but no concept can
   contain an infinite set of representations WITHIN itself.
                                                  ==> INTUITION
        |
        v
CONCLUSION ASSEMBLED, NOT ASSUMED: space is a PURE INTUITION.
        |
        v
THE PARALLEL EXPOSITION FOR TIME
   simultaneity and succession PRESUPPOSE time and cannot yield it
   time cannot be removed from appearances, though appearances can be
     removed from time
   different times are PARTS of one and the same time
   the original representation of time is given as UNLIMITED
TRAP: listing four arguments without SORTING them. Say which pair
   delivers APRIORITY (1-2) and which delivers INTUITIVITY (3-4), and
   the 2019 answer has a structure.

ASCII MASTER FLOW — PANEL 17/74: Transcendental exposition, the two rivals and the double result
==============================================================================
WHAT A TRANSCENDENTAL EXPOSITION IS
   the explanation of a concept as a PRINCIPLE from which the
   possibility of OTHER synthetic a priori cognitions can be understood.
   It PRESUPPOSES the metaphysical exposition and puts it to work.
        |
   SPACE -> GEOMETRY. Geometry determines the properties of space
     synthetically and yet a priori. Possible only if space is a pure
     intuition in us: figures are CONSTRUCTED in pure intuition, and
     what is demonstrated of the construction holds of every object of
     outer sense, since such objects can be given only under this form.
   TIME  -> ARITHMETIC and the PURE DOCTRINE OF MOTION. The concept of
     alteration, and with it motion as alteration of place, is possible
     only through and in the representation of time; NUMBER is generated
     by successive addition of homogeneous units = time-determination.
        |
        v
PYQ 2019 WORDING CAUTION
   "How does Kant argue for the TRANSCENDENCE of Space and Time?"
   "Transcendence" must NOT be read as space and time existing BEYOND
   experience - that is the TRANSCENDENT error Kant attacks. Read it as
   the A PRIORI and TRANSCENDENTALLY IDEAL status, established by the
   metaphysical and transcendental expositions. Say this in ONE sentence
   and then give the expositions.
        |
        v
THE TWO RIVALS AND WHY BOTH FAIL
   NEWTON  space and time are REAL, SELF-SUBSISTENT containers existing
           even if nothing exists in them
           FAILS: two eternal non-entities that are nevertheless
           conditions of everything real, and no experience could ever
           acquaint us with them
   LEIBNIZ space and time are CONFUSED representations of RELATIONS
           among things in themselves
           FAILS: relations abstracted from things could never be known
           with NECESSITY; geometry would lose apodictic status; the
           difference between spatial and conceptual order would be only
           one of CLARITY

ASCII MASTER FLOW — PANEL 18/74: Transcendental exposition, the two rivals and the double result (continued)
==============================================================================
   KANT    neither things nor relations among things, but the FORM OF
           ENCOUNTER - which is why the dispute DISSOLVES rather than
           being decided (the same pattern he repeats for the
           world-whole in the Dialectic)
        |
        v
DOUBLE RESULT - NEVER QUOTE ONE HALF ALONE
   TRANSCENDENTAL IDEALITY  nothing at all if we abstract from the
     subjective constitution of our sensibility -> blocks Newton AND
     Leibniz together
   EMPIRICAL REALITY        within experience objectively valid; every
     appearance without exception conforms -> blocks the charge that
     space is an illusion or a private fancy
   => TRANSCENDENTAL IDEALISM, and NOT Berkeley's idealism; Kant answers
      the charge in the Aesthetic and again at B274-9
HISTORICAL QUALIFICATION: non-Euclidean geometry and relativity weaken
   the inference from ONE privileged Euclidean geometry to a fixed form
   of intuition; they do not refute the general claim that human
   experience has a priori spatio-temporal form, and the METAPHYSICAL
   exposition is untouched.

ASCII MASTER FLOW — PANEL 19/74: Metaphysical deduction - the table of judgements and the categories
==============================================================================
PREMISE: THE UNDERSTANDING IS A FACULTY OF JUDGING. All acts of the
   understanding reduce to judgements, so the FUNCTIONS OF UNITY IN
   JUDGEMENT exhaust the functions of the understanding.
   NOTE: this premise does enormous work. If the understanding did
   anything OTHER than judge, the table could not be complete.
        |
   IN GENERAL LOGIC the FORM of judgement is considered apart from all
   content. IN TRANSCENDENTAL LOGIC the same form is considered as
   applied to a MANIFOLD GIVEN IN INTUITION. The CATEGORY is the concept
   of the SYNTHESIS of that manifold according to the logical function.
   WORKED EXAMPLE: the HYPOTHETICAL form "if p then q" merely relates
   propositions in logic; applied to a manifold given IN TIME it becomes
   the rule "this state follows that one ACCORDING TO A RULE" = the
   category of CAUSALITY AND DEPENDENCE.
        |
        v
 LOGICAL FUNCTION OF JUDGEMENT      ->  PURE CONCEPT OF THE UNDERSTANDING
 QUANTITY                               QUANTITY
   Universal   "All S are P"        ->    UNITY
   Particular  "Some S are P"       ->    PLURALITY
   Singular    "This S is P"        ->    TOTALITY
 QUALITY                                QUALITY
   Affirmative "S is P"             ->    REALITY
   Negative    "S is not P"         ->    NEGATION
   Infinite    "S is non-P"         ->    LIMITATION
 RELATION                        <== THE EXAM GROUP
   Categorical "S is P"             ->    INHERENCE AND SUBSISTENCE
                                          (substantia et accidens)
   Hypothetical "if S then P"       ->    CAUSALITY AND DEPENDENCE
                                          (cause and effect)
   Disjunctive "S is either P or Q" ->    COMMUNITY (reciprocity between
                                          agent and patient)
 MODALITY                               MODALITY
   Problematic "S may be P"         ->    POSSIBILITY - IMPOSSIBILITY
   Assertoric  "S is P"             ->    EXISTENCE - NON-EXISTENCE
   Apodictic   "S must be P"        ->    NECESSITY - CONTINGENCY
        |

ASCII MASTER FLOW — PANEL 20/74: Metaphysical deduction - the table of judgements and the categories (continued)
==============================================================================
        v
WHAT EACH GROUP GOVERNS
   QUANTITY  the EXTENSIVE MAGNITUDE of an appearance - every appearance
             is an aggregate of homogeneous parts, hence measurable
   QUALITY   the INTENSIVE MAGNITUDE of a sensation - every real in
             appearance has a DEGREE, so reality and its absence differ
             continuously, not by jumps
   RELATION  the OBJECTIVE TEMPORAL RELATIONS of appearances - duration,
             succession, simultaneity -> becomes the THREE ANALOGIES
   MODALITY  NOT the object, but the RELATION OF THE OBJECT TO OUR
             FACULTY OF COGNITION; it "contributes nothing to the
             content of the judgement" -> yields the POSTULATES OF
             EMPIRICAL THOUGHT, not a further law of nature
   >>> TRAP FLAGGED EARLY: because modality concerns the relation to
       cognition, NO modal category licenses an inference to a necessary
       BEING. This is exactly the confusion the Fourth Antinomy and the
       cosmological proof exploit.
THE THIRD OF EACH GROUP arises from the combination of the first two:
   TOTALITY = plurality considered as unity | LIMITATION = reality
   combined with negation | COMMUNITY = reciprocal causality |
   NECESSITY = existence given through possibility itself.
   CAUTION: a SPECIAL ACT of the understanding is still required, so the
   third concept is not dispensable.
STATUS SO FAR: these categories are UNSCHEMATISED - pure logical
   functions with NO sensible sense. Until the Schematism supplies the
   schema, a category can be THOUGHT but determines NO OBJECT.

ASCII MASTER FLOW — PANEL 21/74: The two deductions, and the transcendental deduction in six steps
==============================================================================
METAPHYSICAL DEDUCTION            TRANSCENDENTAL DEDUCTION
   QUESTION: WHICH concepts are      QUESTION: by what RIGHT do those
   the pure concepts of the          concepts apply to objects given in
   understanding?  (quid facti)      sensibility?  (quid juris)
   METHOD: derivation from the       METHOD: argument from the necessary
   complete table of logical         unity of SELF-CONSCIOUSNESS
   functions of judgement
   IF IT FAILS: the inventory is     IF IT FAILS: the entitlement is
   wrong or incomplete               missing and the categories are, in
                                     Hume's phrase, bastards of the
                                     imagination
   FORMULA: IDENTIFICATION           FORMULA: JUSTIFICATION
   >>> Confusing the two is a standing UPSC trap and costs a structure
       mark. Empirical concepts pose no quid juris problem because they
       are drawn from the very experience they describe; the PURE
       concepts are exactly those that are NOT so drawn - which is why
       Hume was RIGHT that experience cannot supply them and WRONG about
       the consequence.
        |
        v
THE DEDUCTION, STEP BY STEP
  1 PREMISE (B131-2): "The I THINK MUST BE ABLE TO accompany all my
    representations; for otherwise something would be represented in me
    that could not be thought at all, which is as much as to say that
    the representation would either be impossible or else at least would
    be nothing FOR ME."
    NOTE THE MODAL FORM: not that I actually think "I think" alongside
    each perception, only that I must be ABLE to.
  2 THE UNITY IS ORIGINAL AND TRANSCENDENTAL. Not derived from any
    prior unity; the highest point to which all use of the understanding
    must be attached. ORIGINAL and PURE apperception, distinguished from
    the EMPIRICAL consciousness accompanying particular states.
  3 COMBINATION IS AN ACT. "The combination (conjunctio) of a manifold
    in general can never come to us through the senses." Sensibility is
    RECEPTIVE: it delivers a manifold but never its unity. Every
    combination is an act of SPONTANEITY, exercised by the understanding
    through the PRODUCTIVE IMAGINATION.

ASCII MASTER FLOW — PANEL 22/74: The two deductions, and the transcendental deduction in six steps (continued)
==============================================================================
  4 UNITY REQUIRES RULE. For a manifold to be combined in ONE
    consciousness the combination cannot be arbitrary; it must be
    determinate and repeatable - that is, according to a RULE.
  5 THE RULES ARE THE CATEGORIES. A category IS the concept of a
    synthesis of the manifold in accordance with a logical function of
    judgement.
  6 OBJECTIVE VALIDITY FOLLOWS. An object is "THAT IN THE CONCEPT OF
    WHICH THE MANIFOLD OF A GIVEN INTUITION IS UNITED". To be an object
    AT ALL, for us, is to be a manifold unified under categories.
        |
        v
STRENGTH: the sceptic is asked to concede ALMOST NOTHING - only that his
   experiences are HIS.
RESTRICTION: the conclusion is exactly as wide as its premise - valid of
   objects AS THEY CAN BE GIVEN TO US; things in themselves untouched;
   and it does not show HOW a pure category reaches a temporal
   appearance. That gap is the SCHEMATISM.

ASCII MASTER FLOW — PANEL 23/74: Apperception - what it is, what it is not, and the two flanking errors
==============================================================================
APPERCEPTION IS
   FORMAL | ORIGINAL | TRANSCENDENTAL | A CONDITION | A FUNCTION
   the unity through which a manifold becomes ONE experience; the
   standing possibility that "I think" accompany any representation
APPERCEPTION IS NOT
   an inner observation | an impression | Hume's bundle | a substance |
   knowledge of myself as I am in myself
   NOTE: Kant grants that I know myself only as I APPEAR to myself
   through inner sense - the self is NO EXCEPTION to transcendental
   idealism.
        |
        v
TWO FLANKING ERRORS RULED OUT AT ONCE
  <-- HUME'S ERROR: he looked for the self among IMPRESSIONS and, finding
      none, dissolved it into a BUNDLE. But the "I think" is not an
      impression; it is the FORM of all thinking and therefore cannot
      appear IN experience, being the condition OF it.
  --> RATIONAL PSYCHOLOGY'S ERROR: from the unity of the "I think" it
      infers a SIMPLE, PERMANENT, IMMATERIAL SOUL-SUBSTANCE. But the "I
      think" is a FUNCTION, not a thing; the inference EQUIVOCATES
      between a logical subject and a real substance. This is the
      PARALOGISM.
  KANT'S THIRD OPTION: the self as CONDITION - neither content (Hume)
      nor substance (Descartes). It is not FOUND and not PROVED; it is
      PRESUPPOSED.
        |
        v
RELATION TO SPACE AND TIME  [PYQ 2022 Q1(e) - the marked limb]
   SENSIBILITY supplies the pure FORMS - space and time - IN WHICH a
     manifold can be given at all
   APPERCEPTION supplies the requirement that this manifold be
     COMBINABLE IN ONE SELF-CONSCIOUSNESS
   CATEGORIES are the RULES OF SYNTHESIS through which a manifold given
     in space and time can be represented as an OBJECT for one "I think"
   >>> THE PRECISE CAUTION: the transcendental exposition of space and
       time does NOT itself deduce apperception. The architecture is:
       AESTHETIC gives the forms of intuition -> DEDUCTION justifies the

ASCII MASTER FLOW — PANEL 24/74: Apperception - what it is, what it is not, and the two flanking errors (continued)
==============================================================================
       categories through apperceptive unity -> SCHEMATISM mediates
       their temporal application. Apperception UNIFIES what sensibility
       gives; it neither creates the sensible matter nor proves a soul.
       A script that says "the transcendental exposition proves
       apperception" has INVERTED the architecture.
   WHY THE QUESTION LINKS THEM ANYWAY: the unity is EMPTY without
       something to unify. Space and time deliver a manifold already
       ordered but not yet OBJECTIVELY determined; apperception is the
       DEMAND that this manifold be one experience; the categories are
       the RULES that meet the demand.

ASCII MASTER FLOW — PANEL 25/74: "Understanding makes Nature" - statement, argument, denials, reply
==============================================================================
STATEMENT
   The understanding "makes" nature FORMALLY CONSIDERED, as the
   LAW-GOVERNED UNITY OF APPEARANCES: categories and principles supply
   the a priori rules under which a manifold can count as OBJECTIVE
   NATURE.
ARGUMENT IN THREE LINES
   1 Sensations alone are UNORDERED.
   2 Synthesis under categories - substance, causality, reciprocity -
     yields objects standing in objective temporal relations, and hence
     UNIVERSAL LAWS.
   3 Therefore experienced nature NECESSARILY CONFORMS to the conditions
     of possible experience.
FOUR DENIALS
   NOT the creation of the MATTER of sensation
   NOT the production of PARTICULAR EMPIRICAL LAWS - those are
       discovered
   NOT a claim about THINGS IN THEMSELVES
   NOT PRIVATE IMAGINATION - the constitution is transcendental and
       therefore PUBLIC
THE DISTINCTION THAT EARNS THE MARK
   Kant secures the UNIVERSAL FORM of nature a priori while leaving
   PARTICULAR LAWS to empirical investigation. The dictum expresses
   TRANSCENDENTAL CONSTITUTION plus EMPIRICAL REALISM, not idealist
   creation.
OBJECTION -> REPLY
   OBJECTION: this collapses nature into mind.
   REPLY: transcendental idealism IS empirical realism - appearances are
   PUBLICLY REAL even though known only under our forms of cognition.
        |
        v
SECOND LIMB OF 2018 Q2(b): IS HEGEL'S ABSOLUTISM THE CULMINATION OF
   KANTIAN DUALISM?
   HEGEL ACCEPTS Kant's decisive insight that thought is CONSTITUTIVE
     rather than a passive copy of ready-made reality
   HEGEL RADICALISES it by treating the categories as internally
     SELF-DEVELOPING and contradictions as MOTORS of thought, not merely
     as symptoms of illegitimate transcendence

ASCII MASTER FLOW — PANEL 26/74: "Understanding makes Nature" - statement, argument, denials, reply (continued)
==============================================================================
   HEGEL REJECTS Kant's fixed oppositions - sensibility/understanding,
     phenomenon/noumenon, finite/unconditioned - and the unknowable
     thing in itself
   RULING: a CULMINATION in the sense of EXTENDING constitutive
     idealism, and an ATTEMPTED OVERCOMING in the sense of REJECTING
     Kant's limits and dualisms. Answer PARTIALLY and WITH A
     DISTINCTION, never with a flat yes or no.
   COST/GAIN: the Hegelian gain is SYSTEMATIC UNITY; the cost is the
     stronger claim that dialectical reason can KNOW THE ABSOLUTE.

ASCII MASTER FLOW — PANEL 27/74: Schematism - the problem and the six-step argument for time
==============================================================================
THE PROBLEM
   In every subsumption the representation of the object must be
   HOMOGENEOUS with the concept - the empirical concept PLATE and the
   intuited round dish share ROUNDNESS.
   But NO CATEGORY IS LIKE ANY APPEARANCE. Categories are pure,
   intellectual, non-sensible; appearances are sensible, in space and
   time. They are HETEROGENEOUS.
   The DEDUCTION proved that the categories MUST apply. It did NOT
   explain HOW they CAN apply.
THE SOLUTION
   A THIRD THING (ein Drittes), homogeneous with the category on one
   side and with appearance on the other: IN ONE RESPECT INTELLECTUAL,
   IN ANOTHER SENSIBLE. That mediator is the TRANSCENDENTAL SCHEMA, and
   every schema is a TRANSCENDENTAL DETERMINATION OF TIME
   (transzendentale Zeitbestimmung), produced by the PRODUCTIVE
   IMAGINATION (produktive Einbildungskraft).
   PRECISION: the schema is NOT a mixture, half intellectual and half
   sensible. Time is homogeneous with the category in ONE RESPECT (a
   priori, universal, rule-governed) and with appearance in ANOTHER (the
   form under which every appearance is given).
        |
        v
WHY TIME AND NOTHING ELSE - SIX STEPS
   1 Subsumption requires HOMOGENEITY between the subsuming concept and
     the subsumed representation.
   2 Categories and appearances are HETEROGENEOUS, so a MEDIATOR is
     required.
   3 The mediator must be (a) UNIVERSAL AND A PRIORI, like the category,
     and (b) CONTAINED IN EVERY APPEARANCE, like intuition.
   4 TIME uniquely satisfies both: a pure a priori form (hence
     rule-governed and universal) AND the form of INNER sense, so every
     representation whatever - outer as well as inner - is in time.
     SPACE FAILS (b): outer objects are in space, but my inner states
     are not.
   5 Therefore the mediating schemata are DETERMINATIONS OF TIME
     produced by the transcendental imagination.
   6 Therefore a category has objective application ONLY in and through

ASCII MASTER FLOW — PANEL 28/74: Schematism - the problem and the six-step argument for time (continued)
==============================================================================
     its schema; AN UNSCHEMATISED CATEGORY IS A MERE LOGICAL FUNCTION
     WITH NO SENSE FOR OBJECTS.
FOUR PRESUPPOSITIONS
   P1 Time is the form of INNER sense and therefore of ALL
      representations without exception. If some representation could be
      non-temporal, the bridge does not span.
   P2 Subsumption strictly requires homogeneity. OBJECTION: empirical
      subsumption may not clearly require it either, in which case the
      problem may be over-stated.
   P3 The productive imagination is a genuine THIRD faculty, not
      reducible to sensibility or understanding.
   P4 Categories have NO SENSE apart from schemata - which is precisely
      why the unschematised category can be THOUGHT (the negative
      noumenon) but yields NO KNOWLEDGE.
   >>> P4 is the doctrinally richest: it is the reason Kant can say we
       can THINK things in themselves although we cannot KNOW them.
       Thinking requires only the logical function; knowing requires the
       schema and hence a possible intuition.

ASCII MASTER FLOW — PANEL 29/74: Schema versus image, the four time-aspects, objections and verdict
==============================================================================
SCHEMA IS NOT AN IMAGE - KANT'S OWN THREE EXAMPLES
  CASE      IMAGE (a product)            SCHEMA (a rule / procedure)
  TRIANGLE  any drawn triangle is        the RULE OF SYNTHESIS by which
            DETERMINATELY right-angled   imagination can construct a
            or oblique, equilateral or   triangle IN GENERAL - never
            scalene                      adequate to any single image
  FIVE      . . . . .                    "a METHOD of representing a
                                         multitude in one image" - the
                                         rule of successive homogeneous
                                         addition
  DOG       this particular picture of   the rule by which imagination
            this particular dog          "can delineate the figure of a
                                         four-footed animal in general"
  >>> THE SINGLE MOST EXAMINABLE TRAP: the schema is a RULE/PROCEDURE,
      an image is a PRODUCT. "The schema is a mental picture that
      resembles both" LOSES THE SECTION.
        |
        v
THE FOUR SCHEMATA - FOUR ASPECTS OF TIME (A142-5 / B182-4)
  GROUP      ASPECT OF TIME       SCHEMA
  QUANTITY   time-SERIES          NUMBER - the successive addition of
             (Zeitreihe)          homogeneous units, i.e. the generation
                                  of time itself
  QUALITY    time-CONTENT         DEGREE / intensive magnitude - the
             (Zeitinhalt)         filling of time; the continuous
                                  transition from sensation to its
                                  negation
  RELATION   time-ORDER           SUBSTANCE = permanence of the real in
             (Zeitordnung)        time | CAUSE = succession of the
                                  manifold according to a rule |
                                  COMMUNITY = coexistence of
                                  determinations according to a
                                  universal rule
  MODALITY   time-SUM-TOTAL       POSSIBLE = agreement with the
             (Zeitinbegriff)      conditions of time AT SOME TIME |
                                  ACTUAL = existence AT A DETERMINATE
                                  TIME | NECESSARY = existence AT ALL

ASCII MASTER FLOW — PANEL 30/74: Schema versus image, the four time-aspects, objections and verdict (continued)
==============================================================================
                                  TIMES
  MNEMONIC "SCOR-S": Series - Content - Order - Sum-total
  ARCHITECTURE: four category-groups, four aspects of time, and the
  RELATION row turns DIRECTLY into the three ANALOGIES.
        |
        v
OBJECTION -> REPLY -> RESIDUAL
  1 The schema, being both intellectual and sensible, is as puzzling as
    what it explains - heterogeneity reappears inside the schema.
    REPLY: time is not a MIXTURE but the one form that is a priori AND
    universally instantiated in intuition; homogeneous with each in a
    DIFFERENT RESPECT, not partly each.
    RESIDUAL: Kant's "concealed art" admission concedes the mechanism is
    not exhibited.
  2 HEGEL: the whole apparatus is an EXTERNAL MACHINERY of faculties
    artificially divided and then artificially rejoined; a genuine
    philosophy would show thought and intuition as moments of one
    self-developing concept.
    REPLY: the division is not stipulated but FORCED - sensibility is
    receptive, understanding spontaneous, and no act of will can make
    one do the other's work.
    RESIDUAL: this is the DEEPEST objection and should be flagged in any
    20-mark answer.
  3 The chapter is REDUNDANT given the Deduction (Warren, Paton
    "patchwork" debate).
    REPLY: the Deduction proves THAT categories apply; only the
    Schematism supplies the RULE OF APPLICATION which the Principles
    then deploy - without schemata there could be no Analogies.
    RESIDUAL: the best defence is FUNCTIONAL - the Analogies literally
    ARE the schemata of Relation in operation.
KANT'S OWN CONCESSION: schematism is "AN ART CONCEALED IN THE DEPTHS OF
   THE HUMAN SOUL, whose true operations we can divine from nature and
   lay unveiled before our eyes only with difficulty" (A141/B180-1).
   Renderings vary; attribute with the A/B reference.
EXECUTABLE VERDICT: "The Schematism is not an appendix but the
   LOAD-BEARING JOINT of the Critique: it converts twelve logical
   functions into rules of time-determination and thereby converts the

ASCII MASTER FLOW — PANEL 31/74: Schema versus image, the four time-aspects, objections and verdict (continued)
==============================================================================
   Deduction's abstract entitlement into the concrete Principles. Its
   cost is Kant's own admission that the system's most important
   operation is the one he cannot display."

ASCII MASTER FLOW — PANEL 32/74: The Analogies - master premise, three modes, three principles
==============================================================================
GENERAL PRINCIPLE (B218): "EXPERIENCE IS POSSIBLE ONLY THROUGH THE
   REPRESENTATION OF A NECESSARY CONNECTION OF PERCEPTIONS."
   STATUS: REGULATIVE with respect to INTUITION - they do not tell us
   WHAT we will perceive; CONSTITUTIVE with respect to EXPERIENCE -
   whatever is perceived must be ordered as substance, cause and
   community if it is to count as an OBJECTIVE temporal order at all.
MASTER PREMISE (A176/B219, A183/B226): "TIME ITSELF CANNOT BE
   PERCEIVED." We never see time; we see only things. Therefore every
   determination of objective time-relation must be made BY PROXY,
   through a rule found in appearances.
        |
        v
  ANALOGY   MODE OF TIME    PRINCIPLE                      PROXY
  FIRST     DURATION /      In all change of appearances   the
            PERSISTENCE     SUBSTANCE PERSISTS, and its    persisting
                            quantum in nature is neither   substratum
                            increased nor diminished
                            (B224)
  SECOND    SUCCESSION      All alterations occur in       the causal
                            accordance with the law of     rule
                            the connection of CAUSE and
                            EFFECT (B232)
  THIRD     SIMULTANEITY    All substances, insofar as     mutual
                            they can be perceived as       causal
                            simultaneous in space, are     community
                            in thoroughgoing RECIPROCITY   (commercium)
                            (B256)
        |
        v
WHY EXACTLY THREE: time has exactly THREE MODES and NONE of them is
   perceivable. The three Analogies are not three examples chosen at
   random; they EXHAUST the modes of time. That is also why they are
   RELATION categories and not others.
   >>> Every candidate can list three Analogies. Very few explain why
       there are exactly three and why they are needed at all. The
       master premise answers both.
TRAP: listing the three Analogies without the master premise. Without it

ASCII MASTER FLOW — PANEL 33/74: The Analogies - master premise, three modes, three principles (continued)
==============================================================================
   the principles look like assertions about how the world happens to be
   arranged.

ASCII MASTER FLOW — PANEL 34/74: First Analogy - permanence, alteration and the conservation clause
==============================================================================
NUMBERED ARGUMENT
  1 All appearances are in time.
  2 Time itself cannot be perceived.
  3 Time has two modes to be determined - SUCCESSION and SIMULTANEITY -
    and both presuppose a SINGLE time in which they are located.
  4 To represent things as being in one and the same time, there must
    be, IN THE APPEARANCES THEMSELVES, something that stands PROXY for
    time's own permanence, since time does not change and is not itself
    an object.
  5 That something is SUBSTANCE: the persisting real (das Beharrliche)
    of which all change is mere alteration of determinations.
  6 Therefore coming-to-be and passing-away are NOT the arising and
    perishing of SUBSTANCE but the ALTERATION (Veranderung) of the
    STATES of a persisting substance. WHAT CHANGES ARE ACCIDENTS;
    SUBSTANCE DOES NOT CHANGE, IT *HAS* CHANGING STATES.
  7 Therefore "in all change of appearances substance persists, and its
    quantum in nature is neither increased nor diminished" (B224).
        |
        v
WHY THIS DEFEATS HUME WITHOUT CONTRADICTING HIM
   Hume was RIGHT that there is no IMPRESSION of substance, and Kant
   AGREES ENTIRELY. Kant's move is TRANSCENDENTAL: substance is not
   something perceived IN experience but a CONDITION of perceiving
   anything as located in ONE TIME. The empiricist demand - "show me the
   impression" - is therefore THE WRONG DEMAND, because what is at stake
   is not a content but a condition.
PRESUPPOSITIONS
   P1 time is unitary and unperceivable
   P2 time-determination must proceed through a PERCEPTIBLE PROXY
   P3 "alteration" is intelligible only against a PERSISTING BEARER
STRONGEST OBJECTION
   The argument may prove only that SOMETHING OR OTHER must be treated
   as RELATIVELY persisting for the purpose of time-determination - not
   that there is a sempiternal, quantitatively conserved substance.
   Kant's conservation clause ("its quantum neither increased nor
   diminished") looks like NEWTONIAN PHYSICS smuggled into
   transcendental philosophy.

ASCII MASTER FLOW — PANEL 35/74: First Analogy - permanence, alteration and the conservation clause (continued)
==============================================================================
TRAP: the distinction between ALTERATION of states and COMING-TO-BE of
   substance IS the First Analogy. Losing it turns the argument into a
   bare assertion that things last.

ASCII MASTER FLOW — PANEL 36/74: Second Analogy - the house, the ship, and the adjudication on Hume
==============================================================================
CANONICAL EXAMPLE (A190-3 / B235-8)
  VIEWING A HOUSE      roof -> door -> basement, OR basement -> door ->
                       roof. I CHOOSE the order. REVERSIBLE.
                       = a merely SUBJECTIVE succession of my
                         perceptions of a COEXISTING object
  WATCHING A SHIP      upstream position -> downstream position.
  DRIFT DOWNSTREAM     I CANNOT choose. IRREVERSIBLE.
                       = an OBJECTIVE succession - an EVENT
        |
NUMBERED ARGUMENT
  1 Apprehension of the manifold is ALWAYS successive - in BOTH cases my
    perceptions come one after another. So mere succession-of-
    perceptions cannot by itself constitute the perception of an
    OBJECTIVE succession.
  2 Yet I DO distinguish the house-case from the ship-case.
  3 The distinction cannot be read off time itself, because TIME CANNOT
    BE PERCEIVED (the master premise).
  4 The distinction must therefore lie in the fact that in the ship-case
    the order of perceptions is NECESSARY - B HAD TO follow A and could
    not have preceded it.
  5 Necessity of order cannot come from perception (HUME IS RIGHT ABOUT
    THAT); it can come only from the perceptions being subsumed under a
    RULE that determines the earlier from the later.
  6 Such a rule IS the concept of cause: "everything that happens
    presupposes something upon which it FOLLOWS ACCORDING TO A RULE"
    (A189).
  7 Therefore the causal principle is a CONDITION OF THE POSSIBILITY OF
    EXPERIENCING OBJECTIVE EVENTS - synthetic, a priori, and valid
    necessarily of all appearances, but ONLY of appearances.
PRESUPPOSITIONS: P1 the objective/subjective distinction in temporal
   order is genuine and must be grounded; P2 irreversibility of
   apprehension requires a rule; P3 the only available rule for fixing
   temporal position is causal.
        |
        v
HUME -> KANT, ROW BY ROW
   constant conjunction + custom       -> an A PRIORI CATEGORY

ASCII MASTER FLOW — PANEL 37/74: Second Analogy - the house, the ship, and the adjudication on Hume (continued)
==============================================================================
   no impression of necessary          -> "every event has a cause" is
     connexion, so causation is           SYNTHETIC A PRIORI, necessary
     subjective and psychological         of all possible experience
   induction cannot be justified      -> the causal principle is not
                                         INDUCTIVE but CONSTITUTIVE
   science rests on mere habit        -> science is objectively valid;
                                         its fundamental principles are
                                         the mind's own contribution,
                                         legislating to nature FORMALLY
        |
        v
STRONGEST OBJECTION - NAME IT, THEN ADJUDICATE
   STRAWSON (The Bounds of Sense, 1966): a "NON-SEQUITUR OF NUMBING
   GROSSNESS". Kant slides from (i) the order of my perceptions is not
   up to me - it is determined by the object - to (ii) the event is
   NECESSITATED BY A CAUSAL LAW. Conceptual necessity in the order of
   APPREHENSION does not yield causal necessitation IN NATURE.
   GUYER: at most Kant establishes that we need SOME rule, not the
   UNIVERSAL causal law.
   ALLISON (reply): Kant is arguing about the conditions under which a
   succession of representations can be JUDGED objective, and the only
   candidate rule for fixing an irreversible position in a single time
   is a causal one.
   ADJUDICATION FOR THE ANSWER: Kant defeats Hume at the level of
   CONCEPTUAL ENTITLEMENT - we are entitled to the concept of cause; it
   is not a bastard of the imagination - but he does NOT deliver what
   Hume asked for, namely knowledge of PARTICULAR causal connections.
   That is exactly what Kant intended, since particular laws remain
   EMPIRICAL.
RESTRICTION: valid of APPEARANCES ONLY. The same argument that defeats
   Hume's scepticism FORBIDS inferring God as a first cause - it limits
   scepticism and dogmatism EQUALLY.

ASCII MASTER FLOW — PANEL 38/74: Third Analogy, and the taxonomy of idealism Kant attacks
==============================================================================
THIRD ANALOGY - COMMUNITY / RECIPROCITY
   Simultaneity, like succession, cannot be read off unperceivable time.
   If A and B are simultaneous I can perceive them in either order -
   moon then earth, or earth then moon - but I can also perceive a
   NON-simultaneous pair in either order unless something FIXES them in
   one time.
   What fixes them is MUTUAL CAUSAL INFLUENCE (commercium): substances
   are simultaneous only insofar as they stand in THOROUGHGOING
   RECIPROCAL DETERMINATION. This is the schema of the category of
   COMMUNITY.
   USE: the least examined and the most useful for showing COMPLETENESS
   - it demonstrates that the three Analogies EXHAUST the modes of time
   rather than being three examples chosen at random.
        |
        v
WHY THE REFUTATION OF IDEALISM EXISTS
   1781: the GARVE-FEDER review (Gottingen) presents the Critique as a
   version of Berkeley's idealism in which bodies are reduced to
   representations. Kant regards this as a complete INVERSION of his
   position, since he holds that objects in space are EMPIRICALLY REAL.
   1787: he answers twice - the A-edition FOURTH PARALOGISM is replaced,
   a direct proof is inserted into the Postulates as the REFUTATION OF
   IDEALISM (B274-9), and the second-edition Preface complains that "it
   remains A SCANDAL OF PHILOSOPHY ... that the existence of things
   outside us ... must be accepted merely on FAITH" (Bxxxix n.).
   PROVENANCE: that remark is a FOOTNOTE IN THE PREFACE, not in the
   Refutation.
        |
        v
KANT'S OWN TAXONOMY OF THE ENEMY (B274)
  TYPE          HOLDER      CLAIM
  PROBLEMATIC   DESCARTES   the existence of objects outside us is
  IDEALISM                  DOUBTFUL and INDEMONSTRABLE; only "I am" is
                            indubitable
  DOGMATIC      BERKELEY    matter is IMPOSSIBLE; space and the things
  IDEALISM                  in it are illusory
  DIVISION OF LABOUR

ASCII MASTER FLOW — PANEL 39/74: Third Analogy, and the taxonomy of idealism Kant attacks (continued)
==============================================================================
    BERKELEY is answered by the TRANSCENDENTAL AESTHETIC - space is a
      real a priori FORM of intuition, not a confused perception, so the
      premise of dogmatic idealism collapses.
    DESCARTES is answered by the REFUTATION PROPER.
  ASYMMETRY OF TONE, WORTH ONE LINE: Kant calls problematic idealism
    "rational and conformable to a thorough philosophical mode of
    thought" - it demands proof before assent, which is legitimate.
    Dogmatic idealism he treats as resting on a mistaken premise about
    space.

ASCII MASTER FLOW — PANEL 40/74: Refutation of Idealism - the proof, the presuppositions and the objections
==============================================================================
THESIS (B275): "THE MERE, BUT EMPIRICALLY DETERMINED, CONSCIOUSNESS OF
   MY OWN EXISTENCE PROVES THE EXISTENCE OF OBJECTS IN SPACE OUTSIDE ME."
        |
THE PROOF, NUMBERED
  1 I am conscious of my own existence AS DETERMINED IN TIME - I know
    that this inner state came AFTER that one; inner experience has a
    determinate order. DESCARTES MUST GRANT THIS: his own cogito is a
    DATED episode among others.
  2 All determination of position in time presupposes SOMETHING
    PERSISTENT in perception. [= the FIRST ANALOGY; the two sections are
    a single argument.]
  3 This persistent CANNOT be an intuition IN ME: all my inner
    determinations are exactly what is to be DETERMINED by it; the flux
    of representations cannot supply the fixed point against which the
    flux is measured. Hume's bundle is not a candidate; nor is
    Descartes' res cogitans, since I do not INTUIT a persisting soul, I
    only THINK one.
  4 Therefore the persistent must be something OUTSIDE ME and DISTINCT
    FROM MY REPRESENTATIONS - an actual thing in space.
  5 Therefore consciousness of my own existence in time is AT THE SAME
    TIME immediate consciousness of the existence of things outside me.
    OUTER EXPERIENCE IS NOT INFERRED FROM INNER EXPERIENCE; IT IS A
    CONDITION OF IT.
  6 COROLLARY (B278-9, Note 2): "INNER EXPERIENCE IN GENERAL IS POSSIBLE
    ONLY THROUGH OUTER EXPERIENCE IN GENERAL." Descartes' order of
    certainty is EXACTLY INVERTED.
PRESUPPOSITIONS
   P1 the First Analogy is sound
   P2 I have DETERMINATE, not merely felt, temporal knowledge of my own
      states
   P3 NOTHING INNER can serve as the persistent  <== THIS STEP DOES ALL
      THE WORK, and it is the step an examiner will probe
        |
        v
THREE OBJECTIONS AND THEIR FORCE
  1 The proof establishes at most that I must REPRESENT something as
    persisting and external - not that anything external actually

ASCII MASTER FLOW — PANEL 41/74: Refutation of Idealism - the proof, the presuppositions and the objections (continued)
==============================================================================
    EXISTS. (STRAWSON; GUYER)
    FORCE: very strong; the standard modern verdict.
    REPLY: a merely represented persistent could not do the job, since
    it would itself be an inner determination in flux; the persistent
    must be GIVEN, not posited.
  2 Kant rewrote the proof in a FOOTNOTE in the Preface (Bxxxix n.),
    which suggests dissatisfaction with his own text.
    FORCE: historically true and worth citing.
    REPLY: the revision CLARIFIES rather than replaces - what is
    intuited is the persisting THING, not a persisting REPRESENTATION OF
    a thing.
  3 The refutation defeats Descartes but leaves the thing in itself
    untouched: the "external objects" proved are only EMPIRICALLY
    external, i.e. appearances in space.
    FORCE: correct, and KANT ACCEPTS IT.
    REPLY: this is not a defect but the DOCTRINE - EMPIRICAL REALISM +
    TRANSCENDENTAL IDEALISM. Kant proves the desk, not the noumenal
    desk.
ONE COMPARISON WORTH ONE LINE
   MOORE's "Proof of an External World" (1939) offers a COMMON-SENSE
   REFUSAL of the sceptic's demand ("here is one hand..."). Kant offers
   a TRANSCENDENTAL DERIVATION showing that the sceptic's own premise
   already entails the conclusion he doubts. MOORE BLOCKS the question;
   KANT TURNS it.
EXECUTABLE VERDICT: "The Refutation is Kant's most compressed
   anti-sceptical argument and his most fragile: it succeeds in showing
   that Cartesian inner certainty cannot stand alone, but it delivers
   only EMPIRICALLY external objects. Its permanent contribution is the
   reversal - self-knowledge is parasitic on world-knowledge, not the
   reverse - which is the exact inversion of the Second Meditation and
   the ancestor of Wittgenstein's private-language argument."

ASCII MASTER FLOW — PANEL 42/74: Phenomena and noumena - the boundary, four cautions, the affection problem
==============================================================================
FIRST DISCRIMINATION: APPEARANCE (Erscheinung) IS NOT ILLUSION (Schein).
   The distinction between a real object and an illusory one is drawn
   WITHIN the field of appearances by EMPIRICAL criteria - coherence,
   publicity, lawful behaviour. Transcendental idealism does not make
   the desk less real.
        |
  PHENOMENA (Erscheinungen)        |  THING IN ITSELF / NOUMENAL
                                   |  BOUNDARY
  things as they APPEAR to us,     |  things as they are IN THEMSELVES,
  structured by space, time and    |  apart from our cognitive forms
  the categories                   |
  OBJECTS OF POSSIBLE EXPERIENCE   |  NOT objects of knowledge
  KNOWABLE - science applies here  |  THINKABLE - we can form the
                                   |  concept - but NOT knowable
  the realm of empirical nature    |  freedom is assigned only from a
  and causal law                   |  practical / intelligible
                                   |  standpoint, NOT theoretically
                                   |  cognised
        |
        v
FOUR TERMINOLOGICAL CAUTIONS THAT PROTECT MARKS
  1 THING IN ITSELF (Ding an sich) = an object considered independently
    of our sensible conditions.
  2 NEGATIVE NOUMENON = something considered merely as NOT an object of
    sensible intuition - a LIMITING CONCEPT (Grenzbegriff). LEGITIMATE.
    Its whole function is to FORBID the extension of the categories
    beyond experience. It is possible because of Schematism P4: a
    category can be THOUGHT through its bare logical function even where
    no schema is available.
  3 POSITIVE NOUMENON = an object of NON-SENSIBLE INTELLECTUAL
    INTUITION. Humans possess no such intuition, so this is a
    PROBLEMATIC, not a cognised, concept.
  4 Noumenon and thing in itself OVERLAP in boundary-function but are
    NOT simply interchangeable names for a hidden second object.
  >>> IF THE NEGATIVE IS READ AS THE POSITIVE, Kant appears to assert a
      second world he has no right to assert - and every objection from
      Jacobi to Hegel becomes irresistible.

ASCII MASTER FLOW — PANEL 43/74: Phenomena and noumena - the boundary, four cautions, the affection problem (continued)
==============================================================================
        |
        v
THE LIVE READING DISPUTE - STATE THE SHARED LIMIT BEFORE CHOOSING
   TWO-WORLD  appearances and things in themselves are TWO SETS
   TWO-ASPECT the SAME objects under two epistemic standpoints
   SHARED BY BOTH: we cannot cognise objects apart from our sensible
     conditions.
   VERDICT: Kant's text supports a CONTINUING dispute. State the shared
     limit FIRST, then adopt a reading WITH REASONS - usually two-aspect,
     because it preserves empirical realism and avoids duplicating the
     world while keeping unknowability intact. Do not let the dispute
     displace Kant's own terminology.
        |
        v
THE AFFECTION PROBLEM - JACOBI'S DILEMMA, STATED HONESTLY
   If the categories, ESPECIALLY CAUSALITY, apply only to phenomena,
   Kant cannot straightforwardly say that things in themselves CAUSE our
   sensations. He needs RECEPTIVITY without theoretically describing its
   noumenal source.
   JACOBI: "without the assumption of things in themselves I cannot
   enter the system; with it I cannot remain."
   DEFENSIBLE POSITION, THREEFOLD: (i) the tension is GENUINE and should
   be stated; (ii) Kant's own language is more careful than his critics
   allow - he speaks of the object that AFFECTS sensibility without
   claiming theoretical cognition of a noumenal cause; (iii) on the
   two-aspect reading the pressure is reduced, since affection is the
   receptive aspect of ONE cognitive relation rather than a transaction
   between worlds.
   NOT DEFENSIBLE: writing that "the noumenon is a thing behind
   appearances that causes them" AS THOUGH IT WERE KANT'S OWN DOCTRINE.
   This is THE SINGLE MOST COMMON KANT ERROR IN SCRIPTS.
HEGEL'S CHALLENGE (comparison owned here; the doctrine is Topic 05)
   The unknowable in-itself looks like an EMPTY ABSTRACTION produced by
   FIXING a permanent opposition between appearance and reality. Hegel
   seeks to overcome that fixed boundary through the IMMANENT
   DEVELOPMENT of thought's determinations - NOT by claiming ordinary
   inspection of a hidden object.

ASCII MASTER FLOW — PANEL 44/74: Phenomena and noumena - the boundary, four cautions, the affection problem (continued)
==============================================================================
   KANT'S REPLY: the negative noumenon is a BOUNDARY on sensible
   cognition, not positive knowledge of a hidden object; the "emptiness"
   is DELIBERATE, since a boundary concept with content would no longer
   be a boundary.
   ADJUDICATION: Hegel's objection SUCCEEDS against a KNOWLEDGE-CLAIM
   reading of the noumenon and FAILS against the LIMITING-CONCEPT
   reading.
THREE CONSEQUENCES CARRIED FORWARD
   1 SCIENCE SECURED - everything that can be an object of experience is
     subject to the categories
   2 SPECULATIVE METAPHYSICS CLOSED - soul, world-totality and God are
     not possible objects of experience
   3 FREEDOM BECOMES THINKABLE - natural causality is a law of
     APPEARANCES, so an agent considered as noumenon is not thereby
     determined. THINKABLE, NOT THEORETICALLY COGNISED.
TRAP: "Noumena = Plato's Forms." Plato's Forms are KNOWABLE by reason;
   Kant's noumena are precisely UNKNOWABLE. The resemblance is only that
   both are beyond sense.

ASCII MASTER FLOW — PANEL 45/74: The Dialectic - illusion, the three Ideas, regulative versus constitutive
==============================================================================
TRANSCENDENTAL ILLUSION - WHAT KIND OF ERROR THIS IS
   LOGICAL illusion arises from carelessness and VANISHES once the
   fallacy is pointed out.
   TRANSCENDENTAL illusion does NOT vanish. It is grounded in the nature
   of REASON ITSELF. Even after we understand exactly why the inference
   fails, the pull remains - as the moon still looks larger on the
   horizon to someone who knows the explanation.
   TWO CONSEQUENCES: (i) Kant is not saying metaphysicians were stupid;
   the error is STRUCTURAL. (ii) Critique is PERMANENT HOUSEKEEPING, not
   a one-off cure: reason must keep restraining itself.
        |
        v
THE PRINCIPLE OF REASON
   The UNDERSTANDING unifies appearances under rules; REASON (Vernunft)
   unifies the rules of the understanding under principles. Its supreme
   principle: IF THE CONDITIONED IS GIVEN, THEN THE ENTIRE SERIES OF
   CONDITIONS, AND HENCE THE UNCONDITIONED, IS ALSO GIVEN.
   THE AMBIGUITY THAT GENERATES THE WHOLE DIALECTIC: "is also given"
   read as a DEMAND ON ENQUIRY is legitimate; read as "EXISTS AS AN
   OBJECT" it is not.
        |
        v
THREE IDEAS FROM THREE RELATIONS - NOT AN ARBITRARY LIST
  IDEA        RELATION       OBJECT                DISCIPLINE  PATHOLOGY
  SOUL        CATEGORICAL    the absolute unity    rational    PARA-
  (psycho-    - the subject  of the thinking       psychology  LOGISMS
  logical)    of predication subject; totality of
                             INNER conditions
  WORLD       HYPOTHETICAL   the absolute totality rational    ANTI-
  (cosmo-     - the series   of the series of      cosmology   NOMIES
  logical)    of conditions  conditions in
                             appearance; totality
                             of OUTER conditions
  GOD         DISJUNCTIVE    the absolute unity of rational    IDEAL +
  (theo-      - community of the condition of all  theology    FAILED
  logical)    all possible   objects of thought;               PROOFS
              predicates     totality of ALL conditions

ASCII MASTER FLOW — PANEL 46/74: The Dialectic - illusion, the three Ideas, regulative versus constitutive (continued)
==============================================================================
        |
        v
REGULATIVE VERSUS CONSTITUTIVE - THE GOVERNING DISTINCTION
   REGULATIVE (LEGITIMATE): heuristic guides directing enquiry toward
     SYSTEMATIC UNITY - "seek the soul's unity"; "explain nature AS IF
     it were a complete system"; "think of all existence AS IF grounded
     in a necessary being"
   CONSTITUTIVE (ILLEGITIMATE): they do NOT give theoretical knowledge
     of actually existing objects - no soul-substance, no world-
     totality, no proved God
   THE ERROR: reason mistakes a REGULATIVE Idea for CONSTITUTIVE
     knowledge - and then falls into Paralogisms, Antinomies and failed
     God-proofs.
TRAP: "the Ideas are useless fictions" is as wrong as treating them as
   objects. They are PERFECTLY GOOD INSTRUCTIONS and PERFECTLY BAD
   OBJECTS, and both halves earn marks.

ASCII MASTER FLOW — PANEL 47/74: The Paralogisms and the Ideal of Pure Reason
==============================================================================
THE FOUR PARALOGISMS - rational psychology's inferences from the bare
   "I think"
   1 SUBSTANTIALITY .. the soul is a SUBSTANCE
   2 SIMPLICITY ...... the soul is SIMPLE, hence immaterial
   3 PERSONALITY ..... the soul is NUMERICALLY IDENTICAL over time
                       (personal identity)
   4 IDEALITY ........ the soul's existence is distinct from that of
                       bodies in space
   NOTE: the FOURTH Paralogism IN ITS ORIGINAL FORM is A-ONLY; in 1787
   it is replaced and its work is done by the Refutation of Idealism.
        |
KANT'S REPLY: all four commit the fallacy of EQUIVOCATION
   (Paralogismus) - a FORMALLY FALLACIOUS SYLLOGISM turning on a middle
   term that SHIFTS ITS SENSE between the "I" as the LOGICAL SUBJECT OF
   THOUGHT (a formal condition) and the "I" as a REAL OBJECT GIVEN IN
   INTUITION (a substance).
   WORKED CASE (the first, which generalises): "that which cannot be
   thought otherwise than as subject does not exist otherwise than as
   subject, and is therefore substance." The PREMISE is a truth about
   the FORM OF THOUGHT; the CONCLUSION is a claim about EXISTENCE. The
   "I think" is the VEHICLE of all concepts, and the vehicle cannot be
   one of the items carried.
   AGAINST HUME AND DESCARTES AT ONCE: Hume looked for the self among
   impressions and found none - Kant AGREES, since the "I think" is a
   condition, not a content. Descartes' rational psychology converted
   the same "I think" into a SUBSTANCE - Kant REFUSES, since a formal
   condition is not a real object. The Kantian self is neither a BUNDLE
   nor a SUBSTANCE but a FUNCTION.
   WHAT SURVIVES: the UNITY of apperception is fully real and
   indispensable; what fails is the INFERENCE from it to a KNOWABLE
   SOUL. And I know myself only as I APPEAR to myself through inner
   sense.
        |
        v
THE IDEAL OF PURE REASON - the God-Idea's specific form
  1 Reason seeks the COMPLETE DETERMINATION of every thing: for every
    pair of contradictory predicates, exactly one must belong to it.

ASCII MASTER FLOW — PANEL 48/74: The Paralogisms and the Ideal of Pure Reason (continued)
==============================================================================
  2 Complete determination presupposes a STANDARD - the SUM-TOTAL OF
    ALL POSSIBLE PREDICATES.
  3 Reason represents that totality of positive reality as ONE being -
    the MOST REAL BEING (ens realissimum), the transcendental IDEAL
    associated with God.
  4 It then HYPOSTATIZES this ideal: treats a regulative standard as an
    EXISTING INDIVIDUAL.
  LEGITIMATE USE: guide systematic unity and complete explanation.
  ILLEGITIMATE MOVE: infer that a corresponding BEING EXISTS.
  CONSEQUENCE - THE BRIDGE TO THE GOD-PROOFS: the ONTOLOGICAL proof
    converts the Ideal directly into existence; the COSMOLOGICAL and
    PHYSICO-THEOLOGICAL proofs both reach some necessary or intelligent
    cause and then IDENTIFY it with this most-real being.
  >>> So the Ideal explains WHY all three proofs CONVERGE on the same
      being - and therefore WHY ONE REFUTATION CAN KILL ALL THREE. It
      explains why God becomes reason's natural focus, NOT why
      speculative reason succeeds in proving God.
TRAP: using "paralogism" without naming the fallacy is decoration. Say
   EQUIVOCATION, identify the shifting term, and the answer becomes
   diagnostic.

ASCII MASTER FLOW — PANEL 49/74: Antinomies - when reason enters, and how they are constructed
==============================================================================
WHEN DOES PURE REASON ENTER THE REALM OF ANTINOMIES?  [PYQ 2020 Q3(c)]
   Pure reason enters antinomy when its LEGITIMATE search for conditions
   demands an ABSOLUTELY UNCONDITIONED, COMPLETED world-series and then
   treats that totality AS AN OBJECT IN ITSELF.
   THE ANTINOMIES ARE THE CONSEQUENCE OF *IGNORING* THE PHENOMENON /
   NOUMENON BOUNDARY - NOT A CONSEQUENCE OF MAINTAINING IT CORRECTLY.
   >>> ANSWER FORM FOR 2020: say "culmination of TRANSCENDENTAL REALISM
       ABOUT APPEARANCES", not that Kantian dualism mechanically
       produces contradiction. THE DISTINCTION IS THE CURE, NOT THE
       DISEASE. A script that says "Kantian dualism produces the
       contradiction" has INVERTED Kant's own argument.
        |
        v
HOW THEY ARE CONSTRUCTED - FOUR FEATURES, ALL FOUR MUST BE NAMED
  1 A COSMOLOGICAL IDEA IS SELECTED. Each antinomy corresponds to one
    category-group applied to the world-series: QUANTITY (magnitude in
    time and space), QUALITY (composition), RELATION (causality),
    MODALITY (necessary existence).
  2 BOTH SIDES ARE PROVED INDIRECTLY. Each proof is a REDUCTIO AD
    ABSURDUM of its opposite. Kant gives both proofs their FULL FORCE;
    he does not caricature either.
  3 BOTH PROOFS SHARE A PREMISE: that the world is a GIVEN WHOLE - a
    completed totality of which one of the two contradictory predicates
    must hold.
  4 THE CONFLICT IS EXHIBITED AS NECESSARY, NOT ACCIDENTAL. Given the
    shared premise, BOTH conclusions follow. That is what makes the
    antinomy an argument AGAINST THE PREMISE rather than against either
    party.
  TERMINOLOGY NOTE: "Thesis" and "Antithesis" ARE Kant's own printed
    headings in the Antinomy chapter - safe to use here, unlike the
    triad often misattributed to Hegel.
        |
        v
WORKED EXAMPLE OF THE DOUBLE REDUCTIO - FIRST ANTINOMY
  THESIS PROOF: suppose NO beginning. Then an infinite series of states
    has ALREADY ELAPSED. But an infinite series can never be COMPLETED
    by successive synthesis. Absurd. => THE WORLD HAS A BEGINNING.

ASCII MASTER FLOW — PANEL 50/74: Antinomies - when reason enters, and how they are constructed (continued)
==============================================================================
  ANTITHESIS PROOF: suppose there IS a beginning. Then before it there
    was EMPTY TIME. But in empty time nothing can begin, since no part
    of empty time contains any distinguishing condition of existence
    rather than non-existence. Absurd. => THE WORLD HAS NO BEGINNING.
  DIAGNOSIS: both proofs are INDIRECT and both assume the world is a
    COMPLETED WHOLE about which one of the two must be true. That
    assumption is FALSE: the world-series is given only as a REGRESS
    that can always be continued.

ASCII MASTER FLOW — PANEL 51/74: The four antinomies, the classification and the two resolutions
==============================================================================
  #   THESIS                            ANTITHESIS
  1   The world has a BEGINNING in      The world has NO beginning and NO
  TIME &   time and is LIMITED in       limits in space - it is INFINITE.
  SPACE    space.

ASCII MASTER FLOW — PANEL 52/74: The four antinomies, the classification and the two resolutions (continued)
==============================================================================
        |
        v
THE TWO RESOLUTIONS IN KANT'S OWN LOGIC
  MATHEMATICAL (1 & 2): the error is treating an empirical series that
    is always IN PROGRESS as if it were a FINISHED totality. Since the
    world-whole is never an object of possible experience, calling it
    finite or infinite is EQUALLY SENSELESS. Both sides err by treating
    phenomena as noumena. The regress is INDEFINITE - you may always go
    further, and there is no completed answer to go to.
  DYNAMICAL (3 & 4):
    THIRD ANTINOMY (FREEDOM). In the phenomenal world every event is
      determined by prior causes - THE ANTITHESIS HOLDS FOR PHENOMENA,
      and Kant does not weaken it. But the SAME AGENT, considered as a
      NOUMENON, may possess FREEDOM - a spontaneous causality not
      subject to temporal determination. Kant thus makes ROOM for moral
      freedom alongside natural determinism.
      CAUTION: ROOM, NOT PROOF. Theoretical reason establishes only that
      freedom is THINKABLE WITHOUT CONTRADICTION. "Assigned from a
      practical or intelligible standpoint, not theoretically cognised."
    FOURTH ANTINOMY (NECESSARY BEING). No absolutely necessary being
      need occur AS A MEMBER of the phenomenal series. An intelligible
      ground OUTSIDE that series is not thereby contradicted - but the
      antinomy supplies NO theoretical proof that such a being exists.
TRAP: giving ONE resolution for all four is the commonest structural
   error at 20 marks. Mathematical: BOTH FALSE. Dynamical: BOTH MAY BE
   TRUE. And say WHY - homogeneous versus heterogeneous series.

ASCII MASTER FLOW — PANEL 53/74: Significance of the antinomies, and the limit of the indirect proof
==============================================================================
  1 THEY DEMONSTRATE THAT DOGMATIC SPECULATIVE METAPHYSICS OF THE
    SUPERSENSIBLE CANNOT BE A SCIENCE. Pure reason generates OPPOSED
    conclusions when it treats the world-totality as a knowable object.
    Kant's own critical inquiry into reason's conditions and limits
    NEVERTHELESS REMAINS POSSIBLE - the antinomies do not indict reason
    as such.
  2 THEY MOTIVATE THE CRITICAL TURN. Knowledge must be confined to
    phenomena; but PRACTICAL reason can legitimately POSTULATE freedom,
    God and immortality as conditions of the moral life.
  3 THEY ARE KANT'S MOST DRAMATIC ARGUMENT against pre-critical
    rationalist COSMOLOGY and unrestricted speculative reason.
  4 THE INDIRECT PROOF OF TRANSCENDENTAL IDEALISM. If REALISM ABOUT
    APPEARANCES GENERATES the contradictions and TRANSCENDENTAL IDEALISM
    DISSOLVES them, then idealism is VINDICATED BY ITS CONSEQUENCES.
    A doctrine argued ANALYTICALLY in the Aesthetic and Analytic is here
    vindicated SYNTHETICALLY, by its explanatory yield.
    LIMIT OF THAT PROOF: it succeeds ONLY IF THERE IS NO THIRD
    DIAGNOSIS - and HEGEL SUPPLIES ONE, namely that reason's
    contradictions are PRODUCTIVE rather than PATHOLOGICAL. State this
    limit; it is what converts a strong answer into a distinguished one.
        |
        v
THE 2020 SECOND LIMB, RESOLVED IN TWO REASONS
  REASON 1  The phenomena/noumena distinction is the CURE. Once
    appearances are not things in themselves, the world-series is given
    only as a regress that may always be continued, so the MATHEMATICAL
    antinomies dissolve with both sides false.
  REASON 2  The distinction is what PERMITS the DYNAMICAL resolution:
    natural determination holds of appearances while spontaneous
    causality remains thinkable of the same agent considered as
    noumenon.
  VERDICT: reason enters antinomy by HYPOSTATISING A REGULATIVE DEMAND;
    Kant's dualism RESOLVES the conflict rather than producing it.
TRAP: "the antinomies show that reason is unreliable." NOT QUITE. They
   show that reason, WHEN APPLIED BEYOND EXPERIENCE, generates
   contradictions; within its proper domain (phenomena) reason is
   perfectly reliable.

ASCII MASTER FLOW — PANEL 54/74: God-proofs - the framework and the refutation of the ontological argument
==============================================================================
GENERAL FRAMEWORK
   God (the ens realissimum) lies BEYOND possible experience -> the
   categories (existence, causality) have objective employment only
   through their SCHEMATA and hence only WITHIN experience -> ALL
   THEORETICAL PROOFS FAIL.
   EXHAUSTIVENESS CLAIM - itself an argument: there are only THREE
   possible types of speculative proof. Either one begins from the MERE
   CONCEPT (ontological), or from the EXISTENCE OF SOMETHING
   INDETERMINATE (cosmological), or from the DETERMINATE CONSTITUTION OF
   THE WORLD as revealed in experience (physico-theological). No fourth
   route is available. THIS IS WHAT LICENSES ONE REFUTATION FOR ALL
   THREE.
        |
        v
1  THE ONTOLOGICAL ARGUMENT (Descartes / Anselm form)
   P1 God = the most perfect / most real being.
   P2 Existence is a perfection / a reality.
   P3 A being lacking existence would lack a perfection, and so would
      not be the most perfect being.
   C  Therefore God necessarily exists.
   DESCARTES' IMAGE: existence belongs to God's essence as three angles
   belong to a triangle.
        |
KANT'S REFUTATION - "BEING IS NOT A REAL PREDICATE" (A598/B626)
   * Kant's own wording is closer to "Sein ist offenbar kein reales
     Pradikat" - "BEING is obviously not a real predicate." Write
     "being/existence is not a real predicate (A598/B626)."
   * A REAL predicate (Bestimmung) is one that ADDS TO - DETERMINES -
     the concept of a thing. "REAL" HERE MEANS CONTENT-ADDING, from res;
     IT DOES NOT MEAN "CONCERNING A REAL THING". Using the phrase
     without this gloss is the commonest error in the section, because
     English readers hear "real" as "actual".
   * "Existence" does NOT enlarge the concept. It merely POSITS the
     thing, with all its predicates, as ACTUAL.
   * "A HUNDRED REAL THALERS DO NOT CONTAIN THE LEAST COIN MORE THAN A
     HUNDRED POSSIBLE THALERS." The CONCEPT is identical whether the
     object exists or not. What differs is my FINANCIAL POSITION, not my

ASCII MASTER FLOW — PANEL 55/74: God-proofs - the framework and the refutation of the ontological argument (continued)
==============================================================================
     concept. THE POINT IS NOT THAT MONEY IS IMAGINARY.
   * Therefore one cannot include "existence" among the perfections in
     the concept of God and then argue that God must exist because the
     concept contains existence. The argument moves ENTIRELY INSIDE THE
     CONCEPT and never reaches outside it. However rich the concept, it
     determines only what God would be IF God exists.
   * COROLLARY ON CONTRADICTION: "God does not exist" is NOT
     self-contradictory. If we reject SUBJECT AND PREDICATE TOGETHER,
     nothing remains to contradict. A contradiction arises only if we
     KEEP the subject and DENY one of its genuine predicates - and
     existence is not that kind of predicate.
   PYQ 2021 Q2(c) is exactly this panel; PYQ 2024 Q2(c) is this panel
   PLUS the assessment panel below.

ASCII MASTER FLOW — PANEL 56/74: Cosmological and design proofs, the dependency chain, the postulate
==============================================================================
2  THE COSMOLOGICAL ARGUMENT
   P1 Something exists (at least, I exist).
   P2 The existence of anything contingent requires a NECESSARY BEING as
      its ground.
   C1 Therefore a necessary being exists.
   P3 The only candidate for a necessary being is the ens realissimum.
   C2 Therefore God exists.
   TWO DISTINCT BLOWS
     BLOW 1 (at P2): it MISUSES THE CATEGORY OF CAUSALITY beyond
       possible experience, applying it to the TOTALITY of all
       existents. Causality is SCHEMATISED as succession according to a
       rule IN TIME; the totality of what exists is not an event in
       time, so the schema has no purchase.
     BLOW 2 (at P3) - THE DECISIVE ONE: to identify the "necessary
       being" with GOD, one must assume that the concept of a
       most-perfect being INCLUDES NECESSARY EXISTENCE - WHICH IS THE
       ONTOLOGICAL ARGUMENT IN DISGUISE.
   => The cosmological argument PRESUPPOSES the very argument it was
      supposed to replace. Kant's verdict: a "NEST OF DIALECTICAL
      ASSUMPTIONS" - the pretence of an experiential starting point
      disguises a purely conceptual conclusion.
        |
3  THE PHYSICO-THEOLOGICAL (DESIGN) ARGUMENT
   P1 The world exhibits order, purpose, design.
   P2 This order could not arise from blind matter alone.
   C  Therefore an intelligent designer exists.
   KANT'S RESPECT, FIRST: he calls it the OLDEST, CLEAREST and MOST IN
     CONFORMITY WITH COMMON HUMAN REASON, and says it deserves to be
     named WITH RESPECT. SAY THIS - it distinguishes Kant's position
     from crude anti-theism and shows primary reading.
   THE CEILING: at best it proves a very powerful ARCHITECT who ORDERS
     PRE-EXISTING MATERIAL - NOT an omnipotent CREATOR ex nihilo. The
     evidence is FINITE, so the cause proved is FINITE; the leap to
     INFINITE power and wisdom is not licensed by the data.
   THE BORROWING: to convert "architect" into "infinite, all-powerful
     God" the argument must smuggle in an INFINITE / NECESSARY cause -
     i.e. the COSMOLOGICAL argument - which in turn depends on the

ASCII MASTER FLOW — PANEL 57/74: Cosmological and design proofs, the dependency chain, the postulate (continued)
==============================================================================
     ONTOLOGICAL.
   NOTE: Hume had already pressed the ANALOGY hard in the Dialogues
     concerning Natural Religion. KANT'S OBJECTION IS DIFFERENT AND
     STRUCTURAL - about what the argument can REACH.
        |
        v
THE DEPENDENCY CHAIN - WRITE THIS IN EVERY GOD-PROOF ANSWER
   DESIGN --needs--> COSMOLOGICAL --needs--> ONTOLOGICAL --FAILS-->
   ALL THREE FALL TOGETHER. ONE REFUTATION, THREE CASUALTIES.
        |
        v
KANT'S POSITIVE DOCTRINE - BOUNDED, TWO SENTENCES ONLY
   * Theoretical reason can neither PROVE nor DISPROVE God. Kant is
     THEORETICALLY AGNOSTIC, NOT ATHEISTIC.
   * PRACTICAL reason POSTULATES God: the HIGHEST GOOD - virtue with
     happiness proportioned to virtue - is morally necessary, and its
     realisation requires a being with the power and goodness to ensure
     that proportion. FREEDOM and IMMORTALITY are postulated on parallel
     grounds.
   * "I HAD TO DENY KNOWLEDGE IN ORDER TO MAKE ROOM FOR FAITH" (Bxxx).
     Glaube = "faith" (Kemp Smith) / "belief" (Guyer-Wood).
   * OWNER-SCOPE CAUTION: STOP HERE. The categorical imperative, the
     good will and autonomy are NOT printed in this syllabus item.
ASSESSMENT - REQUIRED BY "CRITICALLY EXAMINE"
   * The objection is widely regarded as DEVASTATING against the
     Cartesian and Anselmian forms, and it permanently changed the shape
     of the debate.
   * CONTESTED: MALCOLM and PLANTINGA argue that MODAL versions - which
     treat NECESSARY EXISTENCE rather than bare existence as the
     relevant predicate - escape Kant's objection. Whether Kant's
     refutation reaches those versions is DISPUTED.
   * SUPPORTING CONSIDERATION: if existence is not a predicate, what IS
     it? Kant's answer - that it is POSITING - was later formalised in
     the logic of the EXISTENTIAL QUANTIFIER, which is why his objection
     is usually judged VINDICATED rather than superseded. This is the
     strongest available CLOSE.
TRAP: "Kant proves God does not exist." WRONG. He proves God's existence

ASCII MASTER FLOW — PANEL 58/74: Cosmological and design proofs, the dependency chain, the postulate (continued)
==============================================================================
   cannot be DEMONSTRATED by theoretical reason, then POSTULATES God via
   practical reason. Agnostic theoretically, never atheistic.
