EVERY ROW IS THE SAME ANSWER APPLIED TO A DIFFERENT QUESTION. That is
   why Kant can CONCEDE Hume's point about impressions while DENYING his
   conclusion, and CONCEDE the rationalists' demand for necessity while
   DENYING its reach.
TRAP: comparing on "reason versus experience". The examinable axis is
   the SOURCE OF NECESSITY, and stating it before the table converts a
   memorised grid into an argument.

ASCII MASTER FLOW — PANEL 61/74: Criticisms, replies and residuals - the three-column discipline
==============================================================================
1 HEGEL: the unknowable in-itself is an EMPTY ABSTRACTION sustained by a
    FIXED appearance/reality opposition.
    REPLY: the negative noumenon is a BOUNDARY on sensible cognition,
      not positive knowledge of a hidden object.
    RESIDUAL: whether a boundary can be drawn without already standing
      on both sides of it remains contested.
2 WHY EXACTLY TWELVE CATEGORIES? The derivation from the table of
    judgements is ARTIFICIAL. (Fichte, Hegel, later Kantians)
    REPLY: the table of judgements is claimed EXHAUSTIVE and SYSTEMATIC,
      and no more complete rival was offered.
    RESIDUAL: the completeness claim depends on the logic Kant
      inherited. DETACH STRATEGY FROM INVENTORY - as Strawson does in
      his austere reading - and the Deduction's core result stands:
      OBJECTIVITY IS A CONDITION, NOT A DISCOVERY.
3 TRANSCENDENTAL IDEALISM COLLAPSES INTO BERKELEY'S SUBJECTIVE
    IDEALISM. (Garve, Feder, 1781)
    REPLY: Berkeley DENIES the existence of external objects; Kant
      AFFIRMS it - objects are EMPIRICALLY REAL and only
      TRANSCENDENTALLY IDEAL. The REFUTATION OF IDEALISM (B274) is
      Kant's explicit answer, and the Aesthetic answers the premise.
    RESIDUAL: none serious; this criticism is DECISIVELY answered.
4 THE AFFECTION PROBLEM. If we cannot know things in themselves, how do
    we know they EXIST or CAUSE our sensations? (JACOBI: "without the
    assumption of things in themselves I cannot enter the system; with
    it I cannot remain.")
    REPLY: Kant speaks of what AFFECTS sensibility without claiming
      theoretical cognition of a noumenal cause.
    RESIDUAL: a GENUINE TENSION; the two-aspect reading REDUCES but does
      not DISSOLVE it.
5 THE PHENOMENA/NOUMENA DISTINCTION OPENS A TWO-WORLD DUALISM AS
    PROBLEMATIC AS DESCARTES'. (Hegel, post-Kantians)
    REPLY: ONE WORLD UNDER TWO ASPECTS - how things appear to us versus
      how they are in themselves - not two worlds.
    RESIDUAL: the textual dispute between two-world and two-aspect
      readings is LIVE.
6 SCHEME AND CONTENT: the system separates an UNCONCEPTUALISED "GIVEN"
    from an IMPOSED conceptual scheme. (later scheme/content critics)

ASCII MASTER FLOW — PANEL 62/74: Criticisms, replies and residuals - the three-column discipline (continued)
==============================================================================
    REPLY: sensible content is ALREADY spatial-temporal, and an object
      exists for cognition ONLY THROUGH SYNTHESIS.
    RESIDUAL: the language of a manifold "GIVEN" to sensibility still
      invites the dualism it denies.
RULE: a criticism with no reply, or a reply with no residual, forfeits
   the assessment mark. Use the three-column discipline every time.

ASCII MASTER FLOW — PANEL 63/74: Graded verdict bank - executable closing positions, part 1
==============================================================================
SYNTHETIC A PRIORI
  10  Kant's third class is a genuine DISCOVERY, not a compromise: it
      names judgements that are INFORMATIVE YET NECESSARY, which Hume's
      fork had declared impossible.
  15  ...its cost is that its EXAMPLES are hostage to science -
      non-Euclidean geometry and relativity removed two showcase cases;
      "7+5=12" survives, "the straight line is the shortest" does not.
  20  The doctrine SURVIVES ITS EXAMPLES. What Kant identified was not a
      stock of privileged truths but a KIND of claim - presuppositional,
      revisable in content, unavoidable in form. Read that way, later
      attacks on the analytic/synthetic distinction damage Kant's
      CLASSIFICATION while confirming his real point, that experience
      rests on commitments not themselves given in experience.
SPACE AND TIME
  10  Neither things (Newton) nor relations among things (Leibniz) but
      FORMS OF INTUITION - the mind's contribution to the possibility of
      appearing.
  15  ...the Transcendental Exposition ties this to the apodictic status
      of geometry; when geometry ceased to be uniquely Euclidean the
      ARGUMENT FROM GEOMETRY lapsed - the Metaphysical Exposition
      (space as a GIVEN SINGULAR WHOLE, not a general concept) does not.
  20  The decisive achievement is NEGATIVE AND PERMANENT: the
      Newton-Leibniz debate is unanswerable because both sides treat
      space as something ENCOUNTERED. Once space is the FORM of
      encounter, the antinomy DISSOLVES - which is precisely the pattern
      repeated for the world-whole in the Dialectic.
CATEGORIES AND THE DEDUCTION
  10  The categories are valid NOT because they mirror reality but
      because without them there could be no unified self-conscious
      experience at all.
  15  ...the argument's STRENGTH is that it needs no premise the sceptic
      can refuse - only that experience is MINE; its WEAKNESS is the
      metaphysical deduction, where twelve categories are read off a
      table of judgements Kant did not himself justify.
  20  The Deduction is the STRONGEST ANTI-SCEPTICAL ARGUMENT in modern
      philosophy and the table of categories its WEAKEST SCAFFOLDING.
      Detach the strategy from the inventory and Kant's core result

ASCII MASTER FLOW — PANEL 64/74: Graded verdict bank - executable closing positions, part 1 (continued)
==============================================================================
      stands: OBJECTIVITY IS A CONDITION, NOT A DISCOVERY.
SCHEMATISM
  10  The schema is a RULE OF TIME-DETERMINATION, not an image; it is
      what converts a logical function into a condition of experience.
  15  ...without schemata the Analogies would be impossible, so the
      chapter is FUNCTIONAL, not ornamental; but Kant's own phrase "an
      art concealed in the depths of the human soul" concedes that the
      mechanism is POSTULATED, not EXHIBITED.
  20  Schematism is where transcendental idealism either EARNS ITS KEEP
      or FAILS: if time-determination genuinely mediates concept and
      intuition, Kant has bridged the rationalist/empiricist divide at
      its deepest point; if Hegel is right that the faculties were
      artificially separated in the first place, the bridge is repairing
      a self-inflicted wound. HONEST VERDICT: the chapter succeeds
      INTERNALLY and remains vulnerable EXTERNALLY.

ASCII MASTER FLOW — PANEL 65/74: Graded verdict bank - executable closing positions, part 2
==============================================================================
ANALOGIES OF EXPERIENCE
  10  The Analogies are the SCHEMATISED CATEGORIES OF RELATION doing
      work: substance secures duration, causality secures succession,
      community secures simultaneity - all because TIME ITSELF CANNOT BE
      PERCEIVED.
  15  ...the Second Analogy answers Hume at the level of ENTITLEMENT to
      the concept of cause, not at the level of knowledge of PARTICULAR
      causal laws; Strawson's "non-sequitur" objection shows the gap
      between ORDER-NECESSITY and CAUSAL NECESSITATION.
  20  The Analogies are Kant's TRUE REPLY TO EMPIRICISM, and the part of
      the Critique most nearly INDEPENDENT of transcendental idealism -
      which is why Strawson could rebuild them without the doctrine of
      appearances. Their permanent result is that OBJECTIVITY IS
      RULE-GOVERNED TIME-DETERMINATION, and that anyone who accepts the
      objective/subjective distinction in temporal order has already
      accepted more than Hume allowed.
REFUTATION OF IDEALISM
  10  Kant INVERTS Descartes: outer experience is not inferred from
      inner experience but is its CONDITION.
  15  ...the proof delivers only EMPIRICALLY external objects, and
      Strawson and Guyer argue it establishes at most a need to
      REPRESENT a persistent - Kant's own footnoted revision (Bxxxix n.)
      suggests he felt the strain.
  20  The Refutation is the moment transcendental idealism turns out to
      be an EMPIRICAL REALISM. Its lasting force is STRUCTURAL: the
      sceptic's own starting point - determinate knowledge of his inner
      life - is shown to PRESUPPOSE what he doubts. Wittgenstein's
      private-language argument is the same manoeuvre without the
      machinery, which is the best evidence that Kant located the right
      pressure point.
ANTINOMIES
  10  The antinomies are NOT contradictions in reason but the SYMPTOM OF
      A FALSE SHARED ASSUMPTION - that the world is a given totality.
  15  ...the MATHEMATICAL antinomies are resolved by declaring BOTH
      sides FALSE (the world-whole is not an object); the DYNAMICAL by
      allowing BOTH to be true IN DIFFERENT RESPECTS - freedom
      noumenally, causality phenomenally.

ASCII MASTER FLOW — PANEL 66/74: Graded verdict bank - executable closing positions, part 2 (continued)
==============================================================================
  20  The Antinomies are the INDIRECT PROOF of transcendental idealism:
      if realism about appearances generated contradictions and idealism
      dissolves them, idealism is vindicated by its consequences. The
      move is POWERFUL BUT NOT DECISIVE - it succeeds only if there is
      no THIRD diagnosis, and Hegel supplies one, namely that reason's
      contradictions are PRODUCTIVE rather than pathological.
GOD-PROOFS
  10  Kant does not DISPROVE God; he disproves the PROVABILITY of God by
      theoretical reason, and then reinstates God as a POSTULATE OF
      PRACTICAL REASON.
  15  ...the core is "being is not a real predicate" - adding existence
      adds no content, so no analysis of a concept can yield existence;
      and since the cosmological and physico-theological proofs both
      terminate in the ontological, ONE REFUTATION KILLS ALL THREE.
  20  Kant's critique clears theoretical metaphysics PRECISELY IN ORDER
      to make room for faith ("I had to deny knowledge in order to make
      room for belief", Bxxx). The result is not atheism but the
      RELOCATION of God from an object of proof to a condition of the
      moral life - a move which secures religion from Hume's scepticism
      at the price of conceding that theology can never be a science.
RULE OF USE: the verdict bank is a CLOSE, never an OPENING. An answer
   that begins with its verdict reads as ASSERTION; an answer that earns
   the verdict and then states it reads as JUDGEMENT.

ASCII MASTER FLOW — PANEL 67/74: PYQ routing ledger 2018-2025 and the answer route for each part
==============================================================================
CORPUS SIGNAL: Kant owns 10 of 112 Western Philosophy question-parts in
   the 2018-2025 corpus. 2025 IS THE ONLY YEAR WITHOUT A KANT-OWNED
   PART. Cross-links do not create duplicate ownership.
 YEAR  PART   MARKS  DEMAND AND ANSWER ROUTE
 2018  Q2(b)   15    "Understanding makes Nature" + is Hegel's
                     Absolutism the culmination of Kantian Dualism?
                     ROUTE: dictum FORMALLY considered -> three-line
                     argument -> four denials -> Hegel accepts /
                     radicalises / rejects -> RULING: culmination in one
                     sense, ATTEMPTED OVERCOMING in another.
 2019  Q4(a)   20    How does Kant argue for the "transcendence" of
                     Space and Time?
                     ROUTE: wording caution (= a priori and
                     transcendentally ideal) -> metaphysical exposition
                     (1-2 apriority, 3-4 intuitivity) -> transcendental
                     exposition (geometry, arithmetic) -> Newton and
                     Leibniz both fail -> ideality WITH reality ->
                     non-Euclidean qualification.
 2020  Q3(c)   15    When does Pure Reason enter the realm of
                     Antinomies? Is it a natural culmination of the
                     Phenomena/Noumena distinction?
                     ROUTE: trigger = the unconditioned read
                     CONSTITUTIVELY -> double reductio of Antinomy 1 ->
                     RULING: NO, it culminates TRANSCENDENTAL REALISM
                     ABOUT APPEARANCES -> two reasons: the distinction
                     dissolves the mathematical pair and PERMITS the
                     dynamical resolution.
 2021  Q2(c)   15    How is the Cartesian formulation of the ontological
                     argument criticised by Kant?
                     ROUTE: Descartes' version -> "being is not a REAL
                     predicate" with the CONTENT-ADDING gloss -> the
                     hundred thalers -> the corollary on contradiction
                     -> verdict: non-provability, not non-existence.
 2022  Q1(e)   10    What is apperception? Discuss with reference to the

ASCII MASTER FLOW — PANEL 70/74: Directive decoder and translation/provenance discipline
==============================================================================
DIRECTIVE DECODER
  "HOW IS X POSSIBLE?" (Kant's own question-form)
     DEMANDS a TRANSCENDENTAL argument: identify the undisputed fact,
     then derive its necessary conditions.
     FORM: given F, and F is possible only if C, therefore C.
     FATAL ERROR: a psychological or causal story about how minds work.
  "EXAMINE / CRITICALLY EXAMINE"  (2022 Q3a; 2024 Q2c)
     ASSESSMENT DOMINATES. For the Antinomies the RESOLUTION is the
     marked part; for the God-proofs the REACH of the objection is.
     FATAL ERROR: a list with no resolution, or a refutation with no
     assessment.
  "DISCUSS"  (2019 Q4a; 2023 Q1a; 2023 Q1e; 2024 Q1b)
     Exposition plus assessment, roughly 60:40. Reconstruct as NUMBERED
     TRANSCENDENTAL STEPS, then one objection with a NAMED SOURCE.
     FATAL ERROR: paraphrasing Kant's vocabulary without argument
     structure.
  "IN THE LIGHT OF THIS STATEMENT" / "ELUCIDATE"  (2023 Q1a)
     LOCATE the sentence in the ARCHITECTURE, then unpack. Name the
     division (Aesthetic / Analytic / Dialectic), the problem it solves,
     and the doctrine immediately before and after it.
     FATAL ERROR: a free-standing definition with no location.
  "HOW FAR DO YOU AGREE?"  (2018 Q2b)
     TAKE A DEFENDED SIDE. Rule in line 1, then defend.
     FATAL ERROR: reporting both views neutrally.
  "GIVE REASONS IN FAVOUR OF YOUR ANSWER"  (2018 Q2b; 2020 Q3c)
     After the ruling, give AT LEAST TWO INDEPENDENT GROUNDS.
     FATAL ERROR: asserting a ruling and then narrating doctrine.
  "HOW DOES KANT RESPOND TO HUME?"  (2024 Q1b)
     Reconstruct BOTH sides and locate the EXACT point of disagreement.
     The point is NOT that Kant found necessity in objects; it is that
     he RELOCATED the question to the conditions of objective
     time-determination (Second Analogy).
     FATAL ERROR: "Kant said causation is a category", with no argument.
        |
        v

  NEVER WRITE: that Kant "proved space and time do not exist", or that
  the noumenon is "a thing behind appearances that causes them". The
  second is JACOBI'S OBJECTION, and stating it as Kant's own doctrine is
  THE SINGLE MOST COMMON KANT ERROR IN SCRIPTS.

ASCII MASTER FLOW — PANEL 73/74: Trap sheet, answer spine and the qualified conclusion
==============================================================================
TEN-LINE TRAP SHEET
  1  transcendental vs transcendent - conditions vs crossing beyond
  2  "a priori" is NOT "analytic" - warrant vs meaning
  3  the Copernican analogy is about the DIRECTION OF EXPLANATION;
     Copernicus DISPLACED the earth from the centre
  4  quote transcendental ideality WITH empirical reality, always
  5  metaphysical deduction IDENTIFIES; transcendental deduction
     JUSTIFIES
  6  apperception is a FUNCTION, not a soul, not Hume's bundle; keep the
     modal "must be ABLE to"
  7  "Understanding makes Nature" = FORM, not matter; UNIVERSAL law, not
     particular laws
  8  schema = RULE; image = PRODUCT
  9  the antinomies: MATHEMATICAL both false, DYNAMICAL both may be
     true; freedom is ROOM, not PROOF
 10  Kant is theoretically AGNOSTIC, never ATHEISTIC; and Kantian ETHICS
     is outside this printed clause
 PLUS: appearance (Erscheinung) is NOT illusion (Schein); noumena are
     NOT Plato's Forms; the Refutation is B-ONLY; "dogmatic slumber" is
     the PROLEGOMENA; never assert noumenal causation as Kant's doctrine

  BODY B  what it EXPLAINS that neither rationalism nor empiricism could
  BODY C  the RESTRICTION - valid of appearances only
  ASSESS  ONE named objection (Strawson / Guyer / Jacobi / Hegel /
          Malcolm-Plantinga), Kant's reply, and the RESIDUAL
  CLOSE   a graded verdict from the verdict bank, EARNED not asserted

QUALIFIED CONCLUSION - THE ONE PARAGRAPH THAT FITS EVERY KANT ANSWER
  Kant's critical philosophy is a single argument with two faces: the
  same demonstration that secures mathematics and the fundamental
  principles of natural science as SYNTHETIC A PRIORI cognition also
  forbids theoretical knowledge of soul, world-totality and God, because
  the forms that make necessity possible reach only what can APPEAR to
  us. Its permanent achievements are the relocation of necessity from
  objects and habits to the CONDITIONS OF OBJECTHOOD-FOR-US, and the
  demonstration that objectivity is RULE-GOVERNED TIME-DETERMINATION.
  Its permanent costs are equally clear: the completeness of the table
  of categories is inherited rather than proved; the affection of
  sensibility by things in themselves cannot be described in the terms
  the system allows; and the boundary between appearance and reality can
  be STATED more securely than it can be EXPLAINED - which is why Hegel
  could plausibly claim that Kant's greatest insight was betrayed by
  Kant's own limits.
